Second transcription's note: The text does not say whether an eligible dependent is actually listed; this reading treats every person who can be claimed as claimed.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 19, Step 4, "Any other person (other than your spouse) who lived with you all year as a member of your household if your relationship didn't violate local law"; p501 p. 13, Kidnapped child. The reply asks dependent.kidnapped of every entry (the project asks it only through dependent.kidnap_residency, months under 7; p501 p. 13: a child who lived with you more than half the year meets the residency test without the kidnapped-child rule) and dependent.lawful of household_member entries (that relationship choice already states "if your relationship did not violate local law"; the project asks lawful only for other and sibling_descendant): 38 completed candidate-unknowns (for example classm.f8962.2.deepseek dep_a, kidnapped; classm.clcw26.2.deepseek dep_b, lawful), the rest partial records. The prior household_member route without qr_all_year no longer gives a value. No completed-return amount differs. executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.json`; 1,565 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,667 partial records; 8,578 comparisons): 8,110 of 8,578 agree; 233 candidate-unknown, 117 project-unknown, 114 project-refuses, 4 candidate-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: 38 candidate-unknown. Project stands.

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: dependent.one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One (this person)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: dependent.one}

  - id: dependent.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}

  - id: dependent.h_one_cent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One cent (used to count a qualifying person)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sc.one_cent}

  - id: dependent.h_ctc_per
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule 8812 line 5 amount per qualifying child ($2,200)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ctc.per_child}

  - id: dependent.h_odc_per
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule 8812 line 7 amount per other dependent ($500)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: odc.per_dependent}

  - id: dependent.h_actc_per
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule 8812 line 16b amount per qualifying child ($1,700)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: actc.per_child}

  - id: dependent.h_aoc_band
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$2,000 (Form 8863 lines 28 and 30)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.full_band}

  - id: dependent.h_aoc_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$4,000 (Form 8863 line 27 limit)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.expense_cap}

  # ---------------- Step 1: qualifying child ----------------
  - id: dependent.h_qc_rel
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Son, daughter, stepchild, foster child, brother, sister, stepbrother, stepsister, half brother, half sister, or a descendant of any of them"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}

  - id: dependent.h_qc_age
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Under age 19 and younger than you (or spouse); or under 24, a full-time student and younger than you (or spouse); or any age and permanently and totally disabled"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.age_limit}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.student_age_limit}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.student, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.disabled, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_qc_res
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lived with you for more than half of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, ge: [dependent.months, {const: 7, type: Count}]}
    note: "The months input already applies the exceptions to time lived with you (temporary absences, birth, death, adoption, placement); 7 or more is read as more than half the year."

  - id: dependent.h_qc_tests
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Meets the conditions to be your qualifying child (Step 1)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_rel, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_age, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.self_support, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_res, {const: true}]}

  # ---------------- Step 4: qualifying relative ----------------
  - id: dependent.h_related
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Related to you (or, on a joint return, your spouse) in a way that doesn't require living with you all year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent_relative"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "in_law"}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.spouse_relative, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_qr_rel
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Member of household or relationship test"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_related, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qr_all_year, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.lawful, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_not_other_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Wasn't a qualifying child of any other taxpayer for 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_claimant, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_nontaxpayer, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_qr_tests
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Meets the conditions to be your qualifying relative (Step 4)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_tests, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_not_other_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_rel, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.gross_income, {param: qr.gross_income_limit}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.supported, {const: true}]}
    note: "The sheltered-workshop exception to the gross income test is not separately modeled; the gross income input is used as given."

  - id: dependent.h_dep_common
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "U.S. citizen, national, resident alien or resident of Canada or Mexico; and you file jointly or can't be claimed as a dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.canada_mexico, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: false}]}

  - id: dependent.h_tie_gate
    printed: "(gate)"
    label: "Qualifying child of more than one person (tiebreaker rules)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_claimant, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_nontaxpayer, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_tests, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_multi_gate
    printed: "(gate)"
    label: "Multiple support situation for a person who is not your qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_tests, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.multiple_support, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_kidnap_gate
    printed: "(gate)"
    label: "Kidnapped child whose time with you may be treated under the kidnapped child rule"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.kidnapped, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.months, {const: 7, type: Count}]}

  - id: dependent.h_claim_tests
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You can claim this person as a dependent (Steps 1, 2 and 4)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_tests, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_tests, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_dep_common, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.claimed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You can claim this person as a dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: dependent.h_claim_tests}
    refuses: [dependent.married, dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.h_multi_gate, dependent.h_tie_gate, dependent.h_kidnap_gate]
    note: "The text does not say whether an eligible dependent is actually listed; this reading treats every person who can be claimed as claimed."

  - id: dependent.h_qc_claimed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Claimed as a dependent as your qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_tests, {const: true}]}

  # ---------------- Step 3 / Step 5: CTC and ODC ----------------
  - id: dependent.ctc_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Child tax credit box (Step 3): TIN, U.S. citizen/national/resident, under 17, and SSNs valid for employment"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: ctc.age_limit}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.work_ssn, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.ctc_ssn, {const: true}]}
    note: "Step 3 question 4 also requires you (or your spouse if filing jointly) to have an SSN valid for employment; read as household.ctc_ssn."

  - id: dependent.odc_person
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Credit for other dependents box (Steps 3 and 5)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.all_tin, {const: true}]}
    note: "Step 5 question 1 (you and your spouse have an SSN or ITIN) is read as household.all_tin."

  - id: dependent.ctc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This child's share of Schedule 8812 line 5 ($2,200)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_ctc_per}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.odc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This dependent's share of Schedule 8812 line 7 ($500)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_odc_per}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.actc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This child's share of Schedule 8812 line 16b ($1,700)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_actc_per}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  # ---------------- Head of household ----------------
  - id: dependent.hoh_test1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Test 1: your parent whom you can claim as a dependent, whose main home for all of 2025 you paid over half the cost of keeping up"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.parent_home_paid, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.hoh_test2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Test 2: lived with you more than half the year and is your dependent (other than only because they lived with you all year) or your unmarried qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, ge: [dependent.months, {const: 7, type: Count}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_tests, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_related, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.married, dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.h_multi_gate, dependent.h_tie_gate, dependent.h_kidnap_gate]
    note: "Items 3 and 4 of Test 2 (married qualifying child; custodial parent under the divorced-parents rule) are covered by refusal on the married and divorced_rule screens."

  - id: dependent.hoh_absence
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Counted an absence that was not temporary (no reasonable return, or home not kept up) toward Test 2"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.hoh_test2, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.absence_no_return, {const: true}]}

  # ---------------- EIC ----------------
  - id: dependent.h_eic_tests
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child for the EIC: relationship, age, no joint return, lived with you in the United States more than half of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_rel, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_age, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.us_half, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_eic_tie
    printed: "(gate)"
    label: "EIC qualifying child of more than one person (tiebreaker rules)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_claimant, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_nontaxpayer, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_eic_tests, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_eic_kidnap
    printed: "(gate)"
    label: "Kidnapped child (Pub. 596 rules)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.kidnapped, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.us_half, {const: false}]}

  - id: dependent.eic_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child for the EIC"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: dependent.h_eic_tests}
    refuses: [dependent.married, dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.h_eic_tie, dependent.h_eic_kidnap]
    note: "A married child (Married child rule) and the divorced-parents rule are refused through the screening inputs."

  - id: dependent.eic_ssn_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC qualifying child who has a valid SSN"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn, {const: true}]}

  # ---------------- Form 2441 ----------------
  - id: dependent.h_dc_under13
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Under age 13 when the care was provided"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {const: 13, type: Count}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_under13, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.dc_qp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying person: your dependent qualifying child under 13, or a dependent not able to care for themselves who lived with you more than half the year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_tests, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_dc_under13, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, ge: [dependent.months, {const: 7, type: Count}]}

  - id: dependent.dc_line2d
    printed: "2(d)"
    label: "Qualified expenses you incurred and paid in 2025 for the person listed in column (a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.dc_paid}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}
    shown_when: dependent.dc_qp

  - id: dependent.dc_line2c
    printed: "2(c)"
    label: "Check here if the qualifying person was over age 12 and was disabled"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, ge: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, ge: [dependent.age, {const: 14, type: Count}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_over12_care, {const: true}]}
    shown_when: dependent.dc_qp

  - id: dependent.h_dc_cent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One cent if a qualifying person, otherwise zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_one_cent}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.dc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying persons this entry contributes (1 or 0)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: percent_floor, part: dependent.h_dc_cent, whole: {param: sc.hundred_cents}, cap: 1}
    note: "No atom turns a Bool into a Count; percent_floor of 1 cent (or 0) over 100 cents with cap 1 yields exactly 1 (or 0)."

  # ---------------- Form 8962 Worksheet 1-2 ----------------
  - id: dependent.h_ptc_counted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Dependent required to file because their income meets the filing threshold"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.ptc_magi_counted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This dependent's modified AGI included on Worksheet 1-2"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_ptc_counted, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.ptc_magi}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_ptc_counted, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  # ---------------- Form 8863 Part III ----------------
  - id: dependent.h_edu_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Education credit can be figured for this claimed dependent (Form 1098-T requirement and student TIN)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_tin_issued, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_edu_over_gate
    printed: "(gate)"
    label: "Tax-free assistance and refunds exceeded an academic period's expenses"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_over, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lines 23 to 26 lead to lines 27 through 30, and the institution's EIN is provided"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line23, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line24, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line25, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line26, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_ein, {const: true}]}
    note: "Without the EIN the American opportunity credit can't be claimed; following the Tip, the student falls to the lifetime learning credit (line 31)."

  - id: dependent.h_llc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Go to line 31 for this student"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc, {const: false}]}

  - id: dependent.h_edu_adj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "AQEE Worksheet line 3: total adjustments (lines 2a, 2b and 2c)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.edu_a2025, dependent.edu_a2026, dependent.edu_refunds]}

  - id: dependent.h_edu_aoc_exp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "AQEE Worksheet line 1 (American opportunity credit): tuition, required fees and course materials"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.edu_inst, dependent.edu_mat]}

  - id: dependent.h_aqee_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "AQEE Worksheet line 4 (American opportunity credit). If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: dependent.h_edu_aoc_exp, take: dependent.h_edu_adj}

  - id: dependent.h_aqee_llc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "AQEE Worksheet line 4 (lifetime learning credit). If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: dependent.edu_inst, take: dependent.h_edu_adj}

  - id: dependent.h_l27
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses, not more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [dependent.h_aqee_aoc, dependent.h_aoc_cap]}

  - id: dependent.edu_line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Don't enter more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_l27}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}
    refuses: [dependent.h_edu_over_gate]
    shown_when: dependent.h_aoc

  - id: dependent.edu_line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Subtract $2,000 from line 27. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: dependent.edu_line27, take: {param: aoc.full_band}}
    shown_when: dependent.h_aoc

  - id: dependent.edu_line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Multiply line 28 by 25% (0.25)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: dependent.edu_line28, rate: {param: aoc.second_rate}}
    shown_when: dependent.h_aoc

  - id: dependent.h_l28_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 28 is zero"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line28, {const: 0}]}

  - id: dependent.h_l29_plus
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Add $2,000 to the amount on line 29"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.edu_line29, dependent.h_aoc_band]}

  - id: dependent.edu_line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "If line 28 is zero, enter the amount from line 27. Otherwise, add $2,000 to the amount on line 29"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_l28_zero, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.edu_line27}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_l28_zero, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_l29_plus}
    shown_when: dependent.h_aoc

  - id: dependent.edu_line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (lifetime learning credit)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_llc, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_aqee_llc}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_llc, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}
    refuses: [dependent.h_edu_over_gate]
    shown_when: dependent.h_llc
```
```
